# aterm-render: spec recovery from its 816 unit tests

> Status 2026-10-16: this file is aterm-render's migration worklist. Each spec in §1 is proved
> through Trust/Clean about the compiled Rust function, and then the tests it subsumes (§2) are
> deleted. The 27 tests in §4 sample no spec; their actions (delete, move to a bench, or restate
> once a fix lands) can be taken now. Nothing in this file has been proved yet.
>
> **Tried 2026-10-16 on seal `150efe4a`: `correction-gate` and `shaping-gate` are blocked, not proved.**
> The new seal removed gap 0, but two other gaps showed up. No code or test was changed.
> - **Gap 9 blocks the whole crate on the strict lane.** `targo trust check -p aterm-render` refuses
>   to compile the unit: `verified Targo refuses unit aterm-render … in-process proc-macro dependencies
>   [aterm-serde-derive] … Evidence-grade verification currently enforces a no-proc-macro TCB boundary`.
>   The macro is not used in this crate. It comes in transitively through aterm-core → aterm-policy →
>   aterm-serde, and Targo's PM-1 walk refuses any proc macro in the loaded closure. The advisory lane
>   (`--allow-l0-gaps`) compiles the crate, but it forfeits proof authority, so its rows do not count.
> - **The proofs themselves go through outside the crate.** In a single-file copy of the three fns
>   (`$HOME/trust-land/seal-aterm-probe/render/main.rs`), `correction_applies` and `should_run_shaping`
>   are each 5/5 proved, `clean-kernel-citation`, `Certified`, `KernelRechecked`. The negative controls
>   go red: `cov != 254` fails the theorem and two rows are refuted, and `||`→`&&` in the shaping gate
>   fails both shaping theorems. Two spelling rules matter. A kernel import inside a module is named
>   with the module path, `trust_import_aterm_render__ligature_shaping__should_run_shaping`. The clause
>   must nest `&&` the way the body does, so write `corrected && (cov != 0 && (cov != 255 && !degenerate))`.
>   The contract text is kept at `$HOME/trust-land/aterm-render-attempt/`.
> - **`shaping_min_run` is also refused by Trust's citation goal builder (filed under gap 3).** For a fn
>   whose parameters are all `bool` and whose result is an integer, the builder types `result` as
>   `Bool`. Each clause form fails: `result == spec(u)` (`TypeMismatch expected Bool, inferred UInt64`),
>   `!u || result == 1` ("an integer literal is not a `bool`"), and `result <= 2` ("`bool` values are not
>   ordered"). `result == (if u { 1 } else { 2 })` is an "unsupported contract predicate expression".
>   The same failures occur with `u64` in place of `usize`.
> - `text_blending::correction_gate_conformance` would not be deleted outright even once
>   `correction-gate` is Certified. Its part (b) checks `blend_text` against the gate on two colour pairs,
>   and its positive control checks that corrected mode really reweights texels. Neither follows from
>   the gate theorem.

Source: `crates/aterm-render` (read only). Spec recovery ran in three chunks over every
`#[test]` fn in `src/` and `tests/` and the production code each one reaches, and the chunk results
were merged here. Nothing was compiled or run; every claim comes from reading the code.

The chunk manifests counted 807 tests. Enumerating `#[test]` fns directly gives **816**: the
manifests undercounted chunk 1 by 6 and chunk 2 by 4, and chunk 3 counted one match in
`tests/render_suite.rs` that is only doc-comment text (that file has no `#[test]` fn). The count
includes `#[ignore]`d benches and the platform-gated (`cfg(windows)`, `cfg(target_os = "macos")`,
`cfg(target_os = "linux")`, `embedded-font`) tests.

Result: **300 specs**, covering **789 tests**. **27 tests are not spec samples** (§4). One spec,
`fire-row-refines-stateless-field`, subsumes no test: it is the seam law the `fire_field` module doc
claims and no test checks (§5).

## 0. Notation used in the statements

Paths in the `subsumes` column are relative to `crates/aterm-render/` (`src/<file>.rs::<fn>`,
`tests/<file>.rs::<fn>`). Paths everywhere else are repo-relative.

Renderers, frames and caches:
- `r`: a `Renderer` reachable from `from_system`/`from_bytes(font, px, theme)` by any sequence of public
  calls (`glyph_key*`, `glyph_image`, `render_input`, `render_input_cached`, `set_*`,
  `seal_admitted_font_sources`, `rebuild_from_admitted`). `Settings(r)` = (font sources, px, theme, pad,
  hinting, synthetic_styles, min_contrast, opacity, variations). `Fresh(r)` = a newly built renderer
  with `Settings(r)`.
- `Paint(r,x)` = `r.render_input(x).pixels` (a fresh frame). `Cached(r,wc,x)` =
  `r.render_input_cached(wc,x).pixels()` through a persistent `WindowCpu` `wc`, where `wc` is reachable
  from `WindowCpu::new()` by `render_input_cached` and `invalidate` calls. `last_damage(wc)` is its
  `DamageOutcome`.
- Settled(r) = r after `debug_block_on_lazy_fallbacks` (no background font parse pending).
  Sealed(r) = r after `seal_admitted_font_sources()`.
- `Dirty(p,c)` = the out-vector of `compute_dirty_rows(p,c,…,&mut dirty)` when it returns `Rows(d)`.
  `reusable(p,c)` = equal rows/cols, (equal `display_offset` or equal anchor `base_y − display_offset`),
  equal `default_bg`, equal wallpaper `Arc` identity, and no double-height row in either frame.
  `rows(S)` = the row tags of an overlay stream `S` (for free sprites, the row union of their signed
  pixel Y-extent). The overlay streams are `cursor_trail`, `cursor_glow_add`, `glow_under`, `char_fg`,
  `ink`, `nova_add`, `fire_patch`, `fire_halo`, `glow_halo`, `rain_quads`/`rain_add`,
  `cat_quads`/`cat_atlas`, `free_sprites`/`free_atlas` and `word_decorations`.
- `Band(r)` = pixel rows `[grid_top + r·cell_h, grid_top + (r+1)·cell_h)`. `Cell(r,c)` = `Band(r)` ×
  `[pad + c·cw, pad + (c+1)·cw)`.

Glyphs and fonts:
- `Cov(face,ch)`: the face's Unicode cmap maps `ch` to a gid ≠ 0 (ttf-parser `glyph_index` is the
  authority). `key(ch)` = `Renderer::glyph_key(ch)` with fields
  `{source: FaceId, glyph_class, ch_or_id, style, px_q, cell_span}`. `Notdef(k)` :=
  `k.source = Primary ∧ k.glyph_class = Mono` (a real primary glyph is `MonoGid`).
- The ink rows of a `GlyphImage` are the bitmap rows with coverage > 0 (Mono) or alpha > 0 (Rgba),
  placed at cell row `baseline − (ymin + height)`. `Q` = 64 (26.6 fixed point).
- Host-dependent specs quantify over the font files the host provides. Their proofs need a font/fs
  model (gap 8) or a certificate over the bundled bytes.

Pixels and blending:
- Pixels are `0xTTRRGGBB`, where `TT` is the transmittance byte. `mul8(c,f) = (c·f+127)/255`.
  `over_rgb(d,s,a)` = per channel `(s·a + d·(255−a) + 127)/255`, keeping the transmittance byte.
  `add_sat(d,s)` = per channel `min(255, d+s)`, keeping `d`'s top byte. `premul_rgb(c,a)` = `c` scaled by
  `a/255`. `L(·)` = BT.709 linear luminance. `EPS` = `TEXT_BLEND_EPS`.

Gaps:
- The `blocking_gaps` column lists rows of the gap table in
  `docs/AUDIT-trust-for-test-migration-2026-10-15.md` §7. **Gap 0** (a seal that publishes Clean rows)
  blocks every spec, so it is listed on every row. A row whose only gap is 0 is provable as soon as the
  `150efe4a` seal is admitted.
- The audit table has no row for **f32/f64 semantics** (the plan lists floats as refused by E6). Specs
  that need floats cite gap 3 as the nearest row. A float-semantics row should be added to the audit;
  77 specs here name f32/f64 in their `rust_features`, and the whole-renderer specs reach floats too.
- Specs bound to a `ty_model!` (LigatureGate, CtFracBearing, DecoBandContainment, PadAbsorption,
  AsymmetricPadLayout, WideCentre, RainBandContainment, DecodeCacheFrameWorkingSet,
  GlyphImageClearAccounting) are proved against the model's invariant directly on the shipping
  function; the conformance tests they subsume go with them.

Cross-crate subjects: `render-input-eq` and `cell-frame-into-equals-fresh` are aterm-core properties
that aterm-render tests happen to check. They are listed here so their tests are mapped, but they belong
in aterm-core's `SPECS.md`. Many render specs take a `RenderInput` built through `aterm_core::Terminal`;
they are stated over every `RenderInput`, so the Terminal is only a test-input generator.

## 1. Specs (TSV)

Columns: `id`, `kind`, `functions`, `statement`, `subsumes` (every test the spec retires; a test
listed under two specs is primary under the first, see §2), `rust_features` (what the proof must
handle), `blocking_gaps` (audit §7 numbers).

## 2. Test → spec map (TSV; 789 of 816)

`primary` is the spec that retires the test. `also` lists the other specs the same test samples. The
789 rows plus the tests in §4 account for all 816 `#[test]` fns.



## 3. Coverage

- Spec kinds: contract 205, equivalence 28, invariant 25, refinement 20, table-property 12, round-trip 4, no-panic 4, other 2.
- The biggest collapses (primary tests per spec): `cached-render-equals-fresh` (31), `glyph-resolution-order` (19), `dirty-rows-sound` (16), `cursor-paint-denotation` (16), `dirty-rows-exact` (13), `procedural-geometry-denotation` (12), `fg-substitution` (11), `procedural-seam-tiling` (10).
- 2 tests are subsumed by more than one spec.

## 4. Tests that are not spec samples

Status 2026-10-16: deleted equality_sees_every_slot, the_old_luminance_rule_disagrees_with_the_chrome, a_raster_is_a_pure_function_of_its_inputs, whole_frame_shift_would_break_chrome, out_of_band_rect_is_rejected, the_laws_catch_the_shipped_seal_regression, dropping_px_from_the_key_reproduces_the_collision, text_only_frame_is_unaffected_by_the_image_path, env_var_restores_font_glyphs_for_box_drawing (its file and `[[test]]` entry too), pad_split_rejects_the_prefix_quantized_frame, edge_hardening_is_load_bearing, bucketed_reference_is_bit_identical_to_the_full_scan, negative_control_a_translucent_ink_would_be_caught and set_px_doubling_clears_cache_and_doubles_height_anchor. Kept for now: a_moved_answer_is_reported (the oracle's only non-vacuity guard; it goes with shaping_answers_hold/reader_answers_hold) and fallback_chain_order_first_element_matches_model_winner (aterm-spec names it as the FallbackPrecedence binding; repoint that first).

| `crates/aterm-render/src/hinted.rs::full_hinting_snaps_the_l_stem_at_12px` | A quality measurement: hinted vs unhinted coverage of 'l' at 12px, counting rows with peak ≥ 240. It pins a visual-quality ratio of a third-party autohinter, not a property of aterm's code. The code-level properties are hinted-raster-shape and hinted-coverage-exact. | **move-to-bench** |
| `crates/aterm-render/src/lib.rs::equality_sees_every_slot` | Theme derives PartialEq/Eq/Hash. Field equality, and equal values hashing equal, is what the derive guarantees: a tautology the compiler enforces. | **delete** |
| `crates/aterm-render/src/lib.rs::text_caches_spread_a_precomputed_colliding_key_set` | A statistical hash-quality property (a precomputed Fx-colliding key set spreads under the per-process keyed hasher with probability > 1 − 2^-70). It depends on a random secret, so it states no deterministic property. The code-level fact (the caches use the keyed hasher, not FxBuildHasher) is a type annotation. | **move-to-bench** |
| `crates/aterm-render/src/lib.rs::the_old_luminance_rule_disagrees_with_the_chrome` | Shows that a retired rule (a linear-luminance closure written in the test) disagrees with the chrome classifier. It samples no shipping code. Once theme-is-dark is proved, its negative control is a mutant of theme_is_dark. | **delete** |

## 5. Implied specs that look wrong, or disagree with the code

1. **Likely code bugs the tests miss**
   - **`vibrancy::is_translucent` disagrees with `bg_quad_alpha` near 1.0.**
     `crates/aterm-render/tests/vibrancy_ink.rs::ink_stays_opaque_under_every_opacity` implies
     `is_translucent(o) ⇒ bg_quad_alpha(o) < 255`. That is false for o ∈ [1 − 0.5/255, 1), about
     [0.99804, 1): `is_translucent` is true, but `(o·255 + 0.5) as u8 = 255`. The i/100 lattice skips this
     window. Either `is_translucent` should test `bg_quad_alpha(o) < 255`, or the rounding should cap at
     254 below 1.0. `vibrancy-alpha` records the gap.
   - **`face_info` reports a cell height the window never draws.**
     `crates/aterm-render/src/bundled.rs::a_configured_font_still_wins_and_the_bundled_families_resolve_by_name`
     pins `face_info` at 10x22 for JetBrains Mono at 16 px, while
     `crates/aterm-render/src/bundled.rs::the_default_face_draws_its_measured_cell_at_16_px` pins the live
     renderer at 10x21. `face_info` computes `ceil(new_line_size + deeper)`, while the renderer rounds via
     `cell_h_baseline`, so `aterm show-face` reports a height the window does not use.
   - **Two family normalizers disagree.** `bundled::normalize` uses Unicode `char::is_whitespace` and
     `to_lowercase`, while `normalize_family` (`src/lib.rs` and `src/font_catalog.rs`) is ASCII-only. A
     family name with non-ASCII whitespace or case resolves as a bundled face but not as an installed
     one. The tests (`bundled.rs::family_names_resolve_to_the_bundled_faces_only_when_active`,
     `lib.rs::normalize_family_collapses_spellings`) sample ASCII only. The specs state each function as
     it is; one of them should change.
   - **`scroll_translate::grid_band_px` can overflow.** It computes `pad + t·cell_h` with
     `saturating_mul` but an unchecked `+`. `band_matches_row_mapping` never reaches that case, so
     `grid-band-px` carries a no-overflow `requires` that the code does not enforce.
2. **Implied specs that are wrong as stated**
   - `crates/aterm-render/tests/leading_law.rs::renderer_geometry_follows_the_law_live` asserts
     `h1 == round(h0 · 1.5)` with `h0` already rounded. The law is `round((a − d + g)·1.5)`, and double
     rounding differs whenever the natural line height is fractional (16.4: `round(24.6) = 25` but
     `round(16 · 1.5) = 24`). It passes only for the fixture's metrics. `leading-law` is stated against
     `cell_h_baseline` of the live metrics instead.
   - `crates/aterm-render/src/fire_field.rs::aa_law_no_hard_silhouette_edges`: the 0.6·cov_cap step
     bound is derived from `aa = cell_h/4` and the root lift `1536/cell_h`, but only `cell_h = 40` is
     exercised. The bound does not follow for small cells (`aa` floors at 3 px), so `fire-aa-lipschitz`
     carries a `cell_h ≥ 40` precondition the test leaves unstated.
   - `crates/aterm-render/src/ligature_shaping.rs::extract_cell_slice_reassembles_wide_raster` passes
     negative cell indices as `k as usize` (wrapping) and relies on `extract_cell_slice` reading `k` back
     `as i64`. The contract takes a signed slice index through a `usize` parameter. Either make `k: i64`
     or keep the wrap explicit in `extract-cell-slice` (it is stated that way).
   - The hdr additive tests (`src/hdr.rs::additive_encode_bounded_by_edr_lattice`,
     `additive_clamp_is_load_bearing`, and the non-vacuity clause of `grid_encode_bounded_and_anchored`)
     go through `hdr_additive_encode`, which is `#[cfg(test)]`-only. The shipping path is the WGSL
     `fs_hdr_glow` twin in aterm-gpu, so these tests bind a CPU float model, not shipped code. Only
     `clamp_add`, `srgb_channel_to_linear` and `sanitize_edr_max` are production functions; the composite
     half of `clamp-add-bounded` should move to a spec of the WGSL twin or be dropped.
   - `crates/aterm-render/src/chrome_metrics.rs::pen_is_drift_free`: its last assertion (the historical
     f32 pen truncated `as i32` shows > 0.5 px error) is a property of deleted code computed inside the
     test. `q-round-half-px` drops it.
3. **Mislabelled or stale tests**
   - `crates/aterm-render/src/fire_field.rs::field_ignores_patch_geometry_and_wraps_seamlessly`: the
     name and doc promise the seam law (a patch split cannot change any pixel; the time ring wraps
     seamlessly), but the body checks only totality and value ranges, as its own comment admits. The real
     property is recorded as `fire-row-refines-stateless-field`, which no test checks.
   - `crates/aterm-render/src/hinted.rs::ink_box_floor_law_ascii_12px`: the name and doc claim the floor
     law (`xmin ≤ every outline x < xmin + width`), but the body asserts only `|cov| = w·h`, advance > 0
     and `w,h ≤ 32`.
   - `crates/aterm-render/src/lib.rs::rgba_keys_resolve_to_colour_emoji_images`: the doc says the Rgba
     placeholder "produces nothing yet", but the body asserts a real, non-empty colour glyph with advance
     2·cell_w. The doc is stale.
   - `crates/aterm-render/src/lib.rs::font_swap_setters_invalidate_stale_glyph_caches`: the comment says
     `set_symbol_fallback_bytes` "today clears NEITHER keys nor glyphs", while the assertions below it
     require both to be cleared.
   - `crates/aterm-render/tests/row_revision_oracle.rs::stamped_dirty_rows_match_the_brute_force_oracle_over_a_mutation_corpus`:
     the "exactness, not merely safety" check `extra <= rows` is vacuous; the over-reported count can
     never exceed the dirty vector's length (rows, or rows + 1 after the strip splice). Its fixture
     `host_sparkle` promises a per-cell animated-ink fg override plus a decoration sprite, but only clears
     `word_decorations`.
   - `crates/aterm-render/tests/synthetic_symbols.rs::mirrored_symbols_are_exact_mirrors` says "exact" but
     allows a per-pixel difference of up to 16. `symbol-coverage-table` states it as a near-mirror within
     one subsample step.
   - `crates/aterm-render/tests/glyph_key_injectivity.rs` (`separates_sizes`, `smallest_px_step`,
     `materialized_spans`) asserts only that keys differing in one field are unequal and hash apart,
     which the derived `PartialEq`/`Hash`/`Ord` already guarantee. The property the file names (a raster
     is a function of its key, so no size is served to another) is never exercised; it is recorded as
     `glyph-key-complete`.
   - `crates/aterm-render/src/lib.rs::glyph_fidelity_proof_catches_mac_roman_substitution`: half of it
     asserts a property of third-party fontdue's enumerated cmap (that it diverges from Unicode on
     Menlo/Monaco). Only the "aterm sides with the Unicode gid" half samples
     `glyph-key-refines-unicode-cmap`.
4. **Tests that mix a spec with a measurement**
   - `crates/aterm-render/src/lib.rs::a_scroll_under_a_chrome_raster_keeps_the_blit_and_matches_a_fresh_frame`
     mixes correctness (warm frame == fresh frame) with an optimization-liveness count (blits ≥ 5 of 6).
     The count is a performance property, not part of `scroll-blit-plan-sound`.
   - `crates/aterm-render/tests/seal_maps_discovered_faces.rs::the_seal_maps_every_discovered_face_and_does_not_grow_rss_by_their_size`
     mixes a contract (copied bytes = 0) with process max-RSS bounds (64 MiB, 16 MiB), and
     `crates/aterm-render/tests/win_heap_attribution.rs::the_font_seal_reads_each_file_once_and_keeps_one_copy`
     has measured live and transient heap caps (96 MiB, 4 MiB). The RSS/heap limbs belong in a bench;
     `seal-admission-residency` states the ownership contract directly.
   - `crates/aterm-render/tests/raster_accuracy_survey.rs::flattening_accuracy_is_bounded`: the pooled-mean
     and row-mean limbs (0.100/255, 0.130/255) are bench thresholds; only the per-cell bound follows from
     `FLATTEN_SAGITTA_PX`. Its own doc says it guards neither the shipped entry point (variation.rs
     crop/map) nor the hinted path. `src/raster_tests.rs::golden_quadratic` and `golden_cubic` are
     self-described re-record alarms over first-party output, not an oracle.
   - `crates/aterm-render/tests/procedural_orthogonal_regression.rs` (both tests) are FNV-hash golden
     corpora re-blessed with `ATERM_BLESS_GOLDEN`. They pin outputs rather than state a property, and
     are retired only once `procedural-geometry-denotation` is stated per family.
5. **Tests that pass vacuously on some hosts**
   - `crates/aterm-render/tests/variation_instantiation.rs`: `sf_mono_resolves_to_regular_not_light`,
     `renderer_binds_one_coord_list_everywhere`, `dark_nudge_applies_gated_and_geometry_stable`, and the
     variable-font halves of `config_requests_apply_and_nonvariable_stays_default` and
     `shaping_variation_cache_tracks_coords` pass without asserting on hosts lacking
     `/System/Library/Fonts/SFNSMono.ttf`. `sf_mono_resolves_to_regular_not_light` also pins an Apple
     font-data fact (default wght < 350), not code.
   - `crates/aterm-render/src/variation.rs::varied_raster_reflects_the_weight_axis` and
     `varied_raster_blank_glyph_is_zero_sized` scan host font directories and print SKIP and pass when no
     suitable font exists.
   - `crates/aterm-render/tests/script_support_matrix.rs::every_script_resolves_to_a_face_that_really_contains_it`
     `continue`s when the picked file is unreadable and never checks code points that resolve to no pick
     (runtime or none).
   - `crates/aterm-render/tests/fallback_coverage_is_truthful.rs::korean_resolves_consistently_to_a_face_that_has_hangul`:
     `homes.len() <= 1` holds vacuously when nothing resolves; it is non-vacuous only where a hard-coded
     Hangul face path exists.
   - `crates/aterm-render/tests/synthetic_symbols.rs::the_colour_silhouette_intercept_draws_the_disc_and_reports_the_text_class`
     and `the_renderer_draws_the_tree_connector_procedurally_even_when_a_font_covers_it`: their key halves
     depend on hard-coded Linux font paths and skip elsewhere.
   - `crates/aterm-render/tests/font_oracle.rs::shaping_answers_hold` and `reader_answers_hold` skip absent
     system faces silently, so on a bare host their equivalence covers committed faces only.
6. **AGENTS.md conflicts and misplaced subjects**
   - `crates/aterm-render/src/font_catalog.rs::the_font_files_memo_is_revalidated_by_the_visited_directories_mtimes`
     sleeps 20 ms so writes land past a coarse mtime tick, which makes it timing-dependent. AGENTS.md
     forbids that. `font-files-memo-coherent` is stated against an injected mtime source.
   - `crates/aterm-render/src/lib.rs::cell_frame_into_matches_fresh_cell_frame` and
     `crates/aterm-render/tests/free_overlay.rs::render_input_eq_compares_sprites_by_value_and_atlas_by_identity`
     test aterm-core functions. Their specs (`cell-frame-into-equals-fresh`, `render-input-eq`) belong in
     aterm-core's `SPECS.md`.

## 6. Proof order (easiest high-value first)

Gap 0 was the only gap recorded on two specs. Trying them on seal `150efe4a` added gap 9 to both,
because the crate's dependency closure includes a proc macro. It also added gap 3 to `shaping-gate`,
because the citation goal builder refuses `shaping_min_run` (see the status note at the top). Gaps 1, 3 and 5 (carriers, body widening, quantifiers) are the
next cheapest, and the integer-only specs in that set are `omega`-class. Floats need a float-semantics
row the audit lacks (§0), so float specs come after the integer ones even when their gap list is short.

1. **`correction-gate`** (gap 9). E6 admits `correction_applies`, and its theorem is Certified in a
   single-file probe (status note). The strict lane refuses the crate because of the proc-macro
   boundary. Once that is lifted, the proof retires part (a) of
   `text_blending::correction_gate_conformance`. Parts (b) and the positive control stay.
2. **`shaping-gate`** (gaps 9 and 3). E6 admits the short-circuit `&&`/`||` of `should_run_shaping`,
   and its theorem is Certified in the probe. `shaping_min_run` waits on the citation goal builder,
   which treats a `bool`→integer fn's `result` as `Bool`. Two pure functions, three tests, and the gate
   that decides whether rustybuzz runs at all.
3. **Integer arithmetic laws** (gaps 3 and 5, no carriers): `wide-center-offset` (refines the WideCentre
   model), `weight-rank`, `premul-add-sat`, `over-premul-law`, `cap-centering-law`, `q-round-half-px`,
   `glow-lerp-law`, `aspect-fit-box`. Each is linear or near-linear integer arithmetic over scalars, so
   it is an `omega` proof once casts, shifts and `min`/`max` are admitted. Together they retire 9 tests
   and are the lemmas the blend, chrome and image specs build on.
4. **Model-bound scalar specs** (gaps 1 and 3): `pad-split` (with its PadAbsorption conformance test),
   `classify-shape` (LigatureGate), `band-clamp`, `clip-span-partition`, `select-face-gate`,
   `slice-key-encoding`, `shade-phase-key`, `procedural-metrics-nesting`, `grid-band-px`. Each returns
   a small struct or enum over integers, and two bind a `ty_model!` directly, so they double as the first
   model→code refinements in this crate.
5. **`resolve-styled-face`** and **`resolve-styled-face-lazy`** (gaps 1, 3, 5, 11, 13). Both are finite
   64-point decision trees, decidable by evaluation once the enum payload and the closure are admitted,
   and they underpin every styled-face rendering spec.
6. **First float specs**, once floats are admitted: `ct-pen-and-bearing` (4 tests, refines
   CtFracBearing), `dark-nudge-gate`, `variation-clamp-axis`, `sanitize-edr`, `clamp-add-bounded`,
   `fallback-scale-clamp`, `fallback-fit-scales`, `shrink-ymin`. Each is straight-line over `f32`.
7. **Pure slice and table specs** (gaps 1, 3, 7): `ink-skip-spans`, `cursor-cutout-cols`,
   `fallback-chain-order`, `frame-raster-cap`, `cursor-rect-geometry`, `deco-resolve-metrics`,
   `scroll-band-translate`, `incoming-strip`, `procedural-coverage-contract`.
8. **The damage-cache theorem**, last: `dirty-rows-sound`, `dirty-rows-exact` and
   `scroll-shift-plan-diff`, then `cached-render-equals-fresh`. It retires the most tests in the crate
   (31 primary, plus 30 more through its three lemmas), but it needs nearly every gap and a model of the whole
   CPU renderer.
